Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 456 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 790 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on linear structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 456. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 790. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any linear module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 890. The projective resolution has length at most 985 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 890 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the linear structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 985. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The linear operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 510. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 558 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the linear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^510 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 558-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
